\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}M\left(\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{N}\right)\mathbf{a}_{M}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{M}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}M^{2}N\left|f_{k}(\mathbf{\Phi})\right|^{2}.\end{array}

The fourth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}M\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}M^{2}\left|f_{k}(\mathbf{\Phi})\right|^{2}.\end{array}

The fifth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(e_{k2}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\sqrt{c_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\mid\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
{{\mathop{=}\limits^{\left(j\right)}}}2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}M\left|f_{k}(\mathbf{\Phi})\right|^{2},\end{array}

where
(j)
uses
\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\right\}=\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}
and then places it at the end of the equation.

The sixth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{e_{k2}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{M}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{M}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M^{2}\left|f_{k}(\mathbf{\Phi})\right|^{2}.\end{array}

The seventh term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{e_{k2}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k1}e_{k2}M^{2}N^{2}.\end{array}

The eighth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{e_{k2}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta e_{k2}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta e_{k1}e_{k2}M\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta e_{k1}e_{k2}M\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right\}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta e_{k1}e_{k2}M\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}(N+1)\mathbf{I}_{N}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta e_{k1}e_{k2}M^{2}N(N+1).\end{array}

The ninth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\delta}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\mid\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}^{2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}^{2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}^{2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}^{2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}^{2}M\left|f_{k}(\mathbf{\Phi})\right|^{2}.\end{array}

The tenth term is

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}2\mathbb{E}\left\{\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}\varepsilon_{k}}\,\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\sqrt{c_{k}}\sqrt{c_{k}}\,\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{A}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\mid\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\varepsilon_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(e_{k1}^{2}M^{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}+e_{k3}M\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\right\}\mathbf{I}_{N}\right)\mathbf{\Phi}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\varepsilon_{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{e_{k1}^{2}M^{2}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+e_{k3}M\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right)\right\}\\
=2c_{k}^{2}\varepsilon_{k}\left(e_{k1}^{2}M^{2}N^{2}+e_{k3}MN\right).\end{array}

Thus, we have completed the calculation of
10
cross-terms. After some direct simplifications, we can obtain
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{k}\right|^{2}\right\}
by combining (
317
) - (
E-C
), (
E-C
) - (
E-C
) and (
E-C
) - (
E-C
) with (
E-C
). With the aid of
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{k}\right|^{2}\right\}
and (
E-A
), we can complete the calculation of the signal leakage
E_{k}^{\rm leak}\left(\mathbf{\Phi}\right)
using (
312
).

Appendix F

Recall the definition of
f_{k}({\bf\Phi})
in (
56
). If
N=1
, we have
\zeta_{1}^{k}=0
. Then, for any design of
\theta_{1}
, we have
\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|=\left|e^{j\theta_{1}}\right|=1
.

If
N>1
, we aim to prove that
0\leq\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|\leq N
. Firstly, by invoking the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|=\left|\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}e^{j\left(\zeta_{n}^{k}+\theta_{n}\right)}\right|\leq\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}\left|e^{j\left(\zeta_{n}^{k}+\theta_{n}\right)}\right|=N.

(475)

The equality holds if the phase shifts of all the RIS elements are aligned as

\displaystyle\theta_{n}=-\zeta_{n}^{k}+{C_{0}},\forall n,

(476)

where
C_{0}
is an arbitrary constant.

Next, we aim to prove that the minimum value of
\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|
is zero. Firstly, if
N
is even, the minimum value
0
is obtained when

\displaystyle\theta_{2i-1}+\zeta_{2i-1}^{k}=\left(\theta_{2i}+\zeta_{2i}^{k}\right)+\pi,1\leq i\leq\frac{N}{2}.

(477)

Otherwise, if
N
is odd, the minimum value
0
is still achievable for

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}\theta_{2i-1}+\zeta_{2i-1}^{k}=\left(\theta_{2i}+\zeta_{2i}^{k}\right)+\pi,1\leq i\leq\frac{N-1}{2}-1,\\
\theta_{N-2}+\zeta_{N-2}^{k}=\frac{\pi}{3},\\
\theta_{N-1}+\zeta_{N-1}^{k}=-\frac{\pi}{3},\\
\theta_{N}+\zeta_{N}^{k}=\pi.\end{array}

Next, we aim to prove that when the phase shifts of the RIS are designed to maximize
\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|
, the corresponding term
\left|f_{i}({\bf\Phi})\right|
for the user
i
is bounded when
N\to\infty
. Note that we can prove this result rigorously under the one-dimensional uniform linear array (ULA) model.
Since the USPA model is only a two-dimensional extension of the ULA model, we can deduce that the conclusion still holds.

By ignoring the elevation direction in (
7
) and (
8
) of the USPA model, we can obtain a one-dimensional ULA model for
\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{a}_{N}
with AoA
\varphi_{kr}^{a}
and AoD
\varphi_{t}^{a}
, respectively. Then, we can rewrite
f_{k}({\bf\Phi})
as

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{l}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\triangleq\mathbf{a}_{N}\left(\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)=\left[1,e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}},\ldots,e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(N-1)\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}}\right]^{T},\\
\mathbf{a}_{N}\triangleq\mathbf{a}_{N}\left(\varphi_{t}^{a}\right)=\left[1,e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}},\ldots,e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(N-1)\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}}\right]^{T},\\
f_{k}({\bf\Phi})=\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}=\sum_{n=1}^{N}e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(n-1)\left(\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}\right)+j\theta_{n}}.\end{array}

With
\theta_{n}=2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(n-1)\left(\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)
, we have
\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|=N
. At the same time, for the user
i
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} f_{i}({\bf\Phi})&=\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}=\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(n-1)\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}\right)+j\theta_{n}}\\
&=\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(n-1)\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}+\sin\varphi_{t}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}\\
&=\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}(n-1)\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}.\end{aligned}

(485)

Then, by using the property of geometric progression, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left|f_{i}({\bf\Phi})\right|&=\left|\sum\nolimits_{n=1}^{N}\left(e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}\right)^{(n-1)}\right|=\left|\frac{1-e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}N\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}{1-e^{j2\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}\right|\\
&=\left|\frac{e^{-j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}N\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}-e^{j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}N\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}{e^{-j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}-e^{j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}\times\frac{e^{j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}N\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}{e^{j\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)}}\right|\\
&=\frac{\sin\left(\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}N\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)\right)}{\sin\left(\pi\frac{d}{\lambda}\left(\sin\varphi_{ir}^{a}-\sin\varphi_{kr}^{a}\right)\right)}.\end{aligned}

(486)

Therefore, if the user
i
does not have the same AoA as user
k
, the term
\left|f_{i}({\bf\Phi})\right|
is bounded when
N\to\infty
. Then, following a similar process, we can prove that the term
\left|\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\right|^{2}
is bounded when
N\to\infty
.

Appendix G

To begin with, we need to derive the first and second order statistical properties for the aggregated channel and the observation vector. The expectation is
\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}=\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta}\,\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}
.
Aided by Lemma
7
, the covariances between
\mathbf{q}_{c,k}
and
\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\operatorname{Cov}\left\{\mathbf{q}_{c,k},\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\mathbf{q}_{c,k}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right\}\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\right)\left(\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{R}_{ris}^{1/2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}^{1/2}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}+\gamma_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\right\}\\
&=\left(\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}\right)\mathbf{I}_{M}.\end{aligned}

(487)

Using Lemma
7
, the definition of
\mathbf{H}_{c,2}
in (
81
), the fact
\mathbf{V}=\mathbf{R}_{emi}^{1/2}\tilde{\mathbf{V}}
, and the independence between channels, noise, and EMI, the covariance of
\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}
is calculated as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\operatorname{Cov}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\right)^{H}\right\}\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}+\gamma_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}+\frac{\mathbf{H}_{c,2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{s}_{k}\mathbf{s}_{k}{}^{H}\mathbf{V}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}^{H}}{\tau p}+\frac{\mathbf{Ns}_{k}\mathbf{s}_{k}{}^{H}\mathbf{N}^{H}}{\tau p}\right\}\\
&=\left(\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\mathbf{I}_{M}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\frac{\beta\delta}{\delta+1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{s}_{k}\mathbf{s}_{k}{}^{H}\mathbf{V}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}}{\tau p}\right\}\\
&\;\;\;\;+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\frac{\beta}{\delta+1}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{s}_{k}\mathbf{s}_{k}{}^{H}\mathbf{V}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}}{\tau p}\right\}\\
&=\left(\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\mathbf{I}_{M}+\frac{\sigma_{e}^{2}\beta\delta\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\sigma_{e}^{2}\beta\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\right\}\\
&=\left(\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\mathbf{I}_{M}+\frac{\sigma_{e}^{2}\beta\delta\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}+\frac{\sigma_{e}^{2}\beta\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\right\}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\mathbf{I}_{M}.\end{aligned}

(488)

Then, the LMMSE channel estimate for channel
\mathbf{q}_{c,k}
is given by

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right\}+\operatorname{Cov}\left\{\mathbf{q}_{c,k},\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\operatorname{Cov}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\left(\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbf{y}_{c,p}^{k}\right\}\right).

(489)

Combining (
489
) with (
487
) and (
488
) completes the proof.

Appendix H

Apply Lemma
7
and
9
, the proof can be done following a similar process as in Appendix
E
. Using the orthogonal property, the noise term is given by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\|^{2}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right\}\\
&=\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}+\gamma_{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\right\}\\
&=M\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\right\}\mathbf{R}_{ris}^{1/2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}\mathbb{E}\left\{\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\right\}\\
&=M\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\left|f_{k}({\bf\Phi})\right|^{2}+\widehat{c}_{k}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{{\bf\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right\}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}{\bf\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}+\gamma_{k}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{{\bf\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right\}.\end{aligned}

(490)

By substituting
\mathbf{H}_{c,2}=\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\delta+1}}\left(\sqrt{\delta}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}+\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\right)
, the EMI term is calculated as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\}=\frac{\beta}{\delta+1}\bigg(\delta\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\}\\
&+2\sqrt{\delta}\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\}\bigg).\end{aligned}

(491)

(
491
) can be derived by inserting the definition of
\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}
from (
86
), using Lemma
7
and
9
, and utilizing the independence between
\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}
,
\mathbf{V}
, and
\mathbf{N}
. Details of the proof are omitted here for brevity.

Next, we discuss the derivation of the interference term. For notational simplicity, define

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}=\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}+\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{k}}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H},\\
&\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}=\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{i}\delta}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}+\sqrt{\widehat{c}_{i}}\tilde{\mathbf{H}}_{c,2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}.\end{aligned}

(492)

Then, based on the independence, the interference term can be divided by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\sqrt{\gamma_{i}}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{i}\right|^{2}\right\},

(493)

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\sqrt{\gamma_{i}}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{i}\right|^{2}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\gamma_{i}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{i}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{i}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}\right\}=\gamma_{i}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\right\|^{2}\right\}=\gamma_{i}E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}},

(494)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\left(\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}+\frac{\mathbf{s}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{V}^{H}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}}{\sqrt{\tau p}}+\frac{\mathbf{s}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{N}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}}{\sqrt{\tau p}}\right)\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right|^{2}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{{\sigma}^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{2}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{{\sigma}_{e}^{2}}{\tau{p}}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\underline{\mathbf{q}}_{c,i}\right\}.\end{aligned}

(495)

By utilizing Lemma
7
and Lemma
9
and following a similar procedure in Appendix
E
, the calculation of interference term can be completed by respectively calculating three expectations in (
495
). The detailed process is omitted to save the space.

Finally, we will tackle the signal leakage term. Recall that
E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{leak}}=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}-\left|\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right\}\right|^{2}
and
\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right\}
has been derived in (
490
). Therefore, we only need to derive
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}
, which can be divided by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\left(\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}+\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right)\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}\\
&+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\frac{\mathbf{s}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{V}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{H}_{c,2}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}}{\sqrt{\tau p}}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\frac{\mathbf{s}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{N}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}}{\sqrt{\tau p}}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\},\end{aligned}

(496)

where

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\left(\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}+\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right)\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left|\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\right|^{2}\right\}+2\operatorname{Re}\left\{\sqrt{\gamma_{k}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\hat{\underline{\mathbf{q}}}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}\mathbf{q}_{c,k}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}\right\}\right\}.\end{aligned}

(497)

The calculation of signal leakage can be completed after obtaining expectations in (
496
) and (
497
). The details are similar to those in the calculation of interference, and therefore is omitted for brevity.

Appendix I

Recall that
\mathbf{\Phi}=\mathrm{diag}\{\bm{c}\}
and
\bm{c}=e^{j\bm{\theta}}
. Then, we can re-express
\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right\}
as

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right\}=\sum_{i}\left[\mathbf{A}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right]_{ii}=\sum_{i}\sum_{a}[\mathbf{A}]_{ia}[\mathbf{\Phi}]_{aa}[\mathbf{B}]_{ai}\left[\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right]_{ii}=\bm{c}^{H}\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\bm{c}.

(498)

Applying the chain rule, the gradient of
\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right\}
with respect to the
n
-th elements of
\bm{\theta}
, i.e.,
\theta_{n}
, can be calculated as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right\}}{\partial\theta_{n}}=\frac{\partial\bm{c}^{H}}{\partial\theta_{n}}\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\bm{c}+\mathbf{c}^{H}\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\frac{\partial\bm{c}}{\partial\theta_{n}}\\
&=-je^{-j\theta_{n}}\left[\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right]_{n}+j\left[\mathbf{c}^{H}\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\right]_{n}e^{j\theta_{n}}.\end{aligned}

(499)

(
499
) is the
n
-th element of
\frac{\partial\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\mathbf{A}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{B}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right\}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}
. Thus, the proof of (
111
) is completed by combining (
499
) to a vector. The proof of (
112
) can be done by noting that
\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{T}\left(\mathbf{A}^{T}\odot\mathbf{B}\right)\bm{c}^{*}\right\}^{*}=\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{A}^{H}\odot\mathbf{B}^{*}\right)\bm{c}=\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{A}\odot\mathbf{B}^{T}\right)\bm{c}
if
\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}
are unitary.

Appendix J

Instantaneous CSI-based schemes need to estimate the cascaded channel and the direct channel in each coherence interval, and then optimize the phase shifts of the RIS in each coherence interval.
In the following, we give a brief introduction of the instantaneous CSI-based scheme in single-user systems, including the system model, channel estimation, problem formulation, and phase shift design.

Assume that only the user
k
exists in the system.
The specific realizations of the channel
\mathbf{H}_{2}
,
\mathbf{h}_{k}
, and
\mathbf{d}_{k}
in the
i
-th coherence interval are denoted by
\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(i)}
,
\mathbf{h}_{k}^{(i)}
, and
\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}
, respectively. Besides, the phase shifts matrix
\bf\Phi
in the
i
-th coherence interval is equal to
\mathbf{\Phi}^{(i)}=\mathrm{diag}\left\{\mathbf{v}^{(i)}\right\}
, where
\mathbf{v}^{(i)}=[e^{j\theta_{1}^{(i)}},\ldots,e^{j\theta_{N}^{(i)}}]^{T}
. Then, the equivalent channel in the
i
-th coherence interval can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathbf{q}_{k}^{(i)}=\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(i)}\mathbf{\Phi}^{(i)}\mathbf{h}_{k}^{(i)}+\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}=\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(i)}\mathrm{diag}\left(\mathbf{h}_{k}^{(i)}\right)\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}.

(500)

Let
\mathbf{G}_{k}^{(i)}\triangleq\mathbf{H}_{2}^{(i)}\mathrm{diag}\left(\mathbf{h}_{k}^{(i)}\right)
represent the cascaded channel in the
i
-th coherence interval.
Next,
the instantaneous CSI-based scheme needs to respectively estimate the
M\times N
cascaded channel matrix
\mathbf{G}_{k}^{(i)}
and the
M\times 1
direct channel vector
\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}
in each channel coherence time.

The estimation of
\mathbf{G}_{k}^{(i)}
and
\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}
can be performed by using a two-phase pilot-based scheme
[
36
]
. In the first phase, the direct link
\mathbf{d}_{k}^{(i)}
can be estimated by using the MMSE estimator, and the needed pilot length is equal to the number of users, i.e.,
1
. In the second phase, using the estimated direct channel, the cascaded channel
\mathbf{G}_{k}^{(i)}
can be estimated by using the LMMSE estimator, and the needed pilot length is equal to the number of RIS elements, i.e.,
N
. Therefore, the overall pilot needed in the considered instantaneous CSI-based scheme is
N+1
. Then, we denote the estimated cascaded channel and direct channel as
\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}
and
\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}
, respectively. The detailed process of the estimation is omitted here, and interested readers can refer to
[
36
, Section
\rm V
]
.

Based on the estimated channels
\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}
and
\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}
, the BS can design the MRC beamforming as
\mathbf{w}^{H}=\left(\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)^{H}
. Then, the received signal at the BS in the
i
-th coherence interval can be expressed as

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}^{(i)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{p}\mathbf{w}^{H}\left({\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right){x}_{k}^{(i)}+\mathbf{w}^{H}\mathbf{n}^{(i)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{p}\mathbf{w}^{H}\left(\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right){x}_{k}^{(i)}+\sqrt{p}\mathbf{w}^{H}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right){x}_{k}^{(i)}+\mathbf{w}^{H}\mathbf{n}^{(i)},

(501)

where
\tilde{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}={\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}-\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}={\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}-\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}
denote the channel estimation errors.

Then, we can express the effective SNR as

\displaystyle\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}\left(\mathbf{v}^{(i)}\right)=\frac{p\left|\mathbf{w}^{H}\left(\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)\right|^{2}}{p\left|\mathbf{w}^{H}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\tilde{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)\right|^{2}+\sigma^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{H}\right\|^{2}},

(502)

and the effective rate of user
k
in the
i
-th coherence interval is given by

\displaystyle{R}_{k}^{(i)}=\left(1-\frac{N+1}{\tau_{c}}\right)\log_{2}\left(1+\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}\left(\mathbf{v}^{(i)}\right)\right),

(503)

where the factor
\left(1-\frac{N+1}{\tau_{c}}\right)
represents the rate loss due to the pilot estimation overhead.

Next, the instantaneous CSI-based schemes need to optimize the phase shifts
\mathbf{v}^{(i)}
in the
i
-th coherence interval. We note that the maximization of
{R}_{k}^{(i)}
is equivalent to the maximization of
\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}
. However, it is challenging to find an optimal solution for the maximization of the SNR when considering the channel estimation error from imperfect CSI. Therefore, we resort to a low-complexity sub-optimal solution which only uses the RIS to maximize the desired signal power. The optimization problem is formulated as follows

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}^{(i)}}\;\;\left|\mathbf{w}^{H}\left(\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)\right|^{2}

(504a)

\displaystyle\text{ s.t. }\quad 0\leq\theta_{n}^{(i)}<2\pi,\forall n.

(504b)

A closed-from solution for the problem in (
504
) can be obtained by using alternating optimization
[
10
, Section
\rm III
]
. First, given the phase shifts vector
\mathbf{v}^{(i)}
, the MRC beamforming vector is set to
\mathbf{w}^{H}=\left(\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}+\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)^{H}
. Then, given the MRC beamforming vector
\mathbf{w}^{H}
, the RIS phase shifts are optimized by aligning the phase of the cascaded channel with the phase of the direct channel, i.e.,

\mathrm{arg}\left(\mathbf{w}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{G}}_{k}^{(i)}\mathbf{v}^{(i)}\right)=\mathrm{arg}\left(\mathbf{w}^{H}\hat{\mathbf{d}}_{k}^{(i)}\right)
.
Then, the solution
\mathbf{v}^{*(i)}
is obtained when the alternating optimization algorithm reaches convergence. Based on the optimized solution
\mathbf{v}^{*(i)}
, the achievable rate in the
i
-th coherence interval is obtained as
{R}_{k}^{*(i)}=\left(1-\frac{N+1}{\tau_{c}}\right)\log_{2}\left(1+\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}\left(\mathbf{v}^{*(i)}\right)\right)
.

Finally, by repeating the above procedure for
T_{ci}
coherence intervals, the average achievable rate for the instantaneous CSI-based scheme is given by

\displaystyle\overline{R}_{k}^{*}=\left(1-\frac{N+1}{\tau_{c}}\right)\frac{1}{T_{ci}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{ci}}\log_{2}\left(1+\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}\left(\mathbf{v}^{*(i)}\right)\right).

(505)

The rate in (
505
), which consists of a rate loss factor equal to
1-\frac{N+1}{\tau_{c}}
, is plotted in Fig.
4
in Section
VII
. It is apparent that the rate in (
505
) is negatively affected by the channel estimation overhead. If
N+1>\tau_{c}
, the rate reduces to zero, since all the symbols in the coherence interval are used for pilot transmission, and no symbol is left for data transmission. To gain more insights, we consider to replace the rate loss factor in (
505
) with
1-\frac{1}{\tau_{c}}
, which is the same as that in the proposed two-timescale scheme. In this case, the rate is given by

\displaystyle\overline{R}_{k}^{*}=\left(1-\frac{1}{\tau_{c}}\right)\frac{1}{T_{ci}}\sum_{i=1}^{T_{ci}}\log_{2}\left(1+\mathrm{SNR}_{k}^{(i)}\left(\mathbf{v}^{*(i)}\right)\right).

(506)

The rate in (
506
), which is, however, not achievable, is plotted in Fig.
4
in Section
VII
. Compared with (
505
), the only difference in (
506
) is that the additional, but necessary, channel estimation overhead is ignored.

Appendix K
Expressions for Gradient Vectors

Theorem 6
.

The gradient of
f(\bm{\theta})
with respect to
\bm{\theta}
is given by

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\tau^{o}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\{\frac{\exp\left\{-\mu\underline{R}_{k}(\bm{\theta})\right\}}{1+\operatorname{SINR}_{k}(\bm{\theta})}\frac{\partial\operatorname{SINR}_{k}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\right\}}{(\ln 2)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\exp\left\{-\mu\underline{R}_{k}(\bm{\theta})\right\}\right)},

(507)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial\operatorname{SINR}_{k}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{p\frac{\partial E_{k}^{\mathrm{signal}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}}{pE_{k}^{\mathrm{leak}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}I_{ki}+\sigma^{2}E_{k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}-pE_{k}^{\mathrm{signal}}\frac{p\frac{\partial E_{k}^{\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}\frac{\partial I_{ki}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+\sigma^{2}\frac{\partial E_{k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}}{\left(pE_{k}^{\mathrm{leak}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}I_{ki}+\sigma^{2}E_{k}^{\mathrm{noise}}\right)^{2}}.\end{aligned}

(508)

where

\displaystyle E_{k}^{(\mathrm{signal})}=2E_{k}^{(\mathrm{noise})}\frac{\partial E_{k}^{(\mathrm{noise})}}{\partial\bm{\theta}},

(509)

\displaystyle E_{k}^{(\mathrm{noise})}=(Mc_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k})\frac{\partial\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}},

(510)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\partial I_{ki}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}&=s_{ki5}\frac{\partial\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\left|f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}+s_{ki5}\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}\frac{\partial\left|f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&+s_{ki6}\frac{\partial\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+s_{ki7}\frac{\partial\left|f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+s_{ki8}\frac{\partial f_{k}^{H}(\bm{\Phi})f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+s_{ki9}\frac{\partial f_{i}^{H}(\bm{\Phi})f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})}{\partial\bm{\theta}},\end{aligned}

(511)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{k}^{(\mathrm{leakage})}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=s_{k11}\frac{\partial\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}},

(512)

with

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=2\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\odot\left(\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right\},\\
&\frac{\partial\left|f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=2\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\odot\left(\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right\},\\
&\frac{\partial f_{k}^{H}(\bm{\Phi})f_{i}(\bm{\Phi})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right),\\
&\frac{\partial f_{i}^{H}(\bm{\Phi})f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right),\end{aligned}

(513)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &s_{ki5}=M^{2}c_{k}c_{i}\delta^{2}\varepsilon_{k}\varepsilon_{i},\\
&s_{ki6}=Mc_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\left\{c_{i}\left(MN\delta+N\varepsilon_{i}+N+2Me_{k1}\right)+\gamma_{i}\right\},\\
&s_{ki7}=Mc_{i}\delta\varepsilon_{i}\left\{c_{k}e_{k2}\left(MN\delta e_{k2}+N\varepsilon_{k}e_{k2}+Ne_{k2}+2Me_{k1}\right)+\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)e_{k2}^{2}\right\},\\
&s_{ki8}=M^{2}c_{k}c_{i}\varepsilon_{k}\varepsilon_{i}e_{k1}\delta\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k},\\
&s_{ki9}=M^{2}c_{k}c_{i}\varepsilon_{k}\varepsilon_{i}e_{k1}\delta\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i},\\
&s_{k11}=Mc_{k}^{2}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\left\{N\left(M\delta+\varepsilon_{k}+1\right)\left(e_{k2}{}^{2}+1\right)+2\left(Me_{k1}+e_{k2}\right)\left(e_{k2}+1\right)\right\}\\
&+Mc_{k}\delta\varepsilon_{k}\left(\gamma_{k}+\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)e_{k2}^{2}\right),\end{aligned}

(514)

Theorem 7
.

The gradient of
f_{c}(\bm{\theta})
with respect to
\bm{\theta}
is given by

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{c}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\tau^{o}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\{\frac{\exp\left\{-\mu\underline{R}_{c,k}(\bm{\theta})\right\}}{1+\operatorname{SINR}_{c,k}(\bm{\theta})}\frac{\partial\operatorname{SINR}_{c,k}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\right\}}{(\ln 2)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\exp\left\{-\mu\underline{R}_{c,k}(\bm{\theta})\right\}\right)},

(515)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial\operatorname{SINR}_{c,k}(\bm{\theta})}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{p\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{signal}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}}{pE_{c,k}^{\mathrm{leak}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}I_{c,ki}+\sigma_{e}^{2}E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{emi}}+\sigma^{2}E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}\\
&-pE_{c,k}^{\mathrm{signal}}\frac{p\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+\sigma_{e}^{2}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{emi}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+\sigma^{2}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}}{\left(pE_{c,k}^{\mathrm{leak}}+p\sum_{i=1,i\neq k}^{K}I_{c,ki}+\sigma_{e}^{2}E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{emi}}+\sigma^{2}E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}\right)^{2}}.\end{aligned}

(516)

The gradient of signal is

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{signal}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\partial\left\{\left(E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}\right)^{2}\right\}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=2E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}},

(517)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=M\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{c})+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+\gamma_{k}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)+\widehat{c}_{k}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{c}).

(518)

The gradient of EMI is
\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\beta}{\delta+1}\sum_{\omega=1}^{8}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\omega,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}
, where

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{1,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}&=M^{2}\widehat{c}_{k}\delta^{2}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{a}_{N}\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\\
&+2M^{2}\widehat{c}_{k}\delta^{2}f_{c,k,7}(\bm{\Phi})\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{a}_{N}\mathbf{a}_{N}^{H}\odot\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\bm{c}\right\},\end{aligned}

(519)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{2,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}&=\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\frac{2\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,3}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{2\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})+\delta\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,3}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(520)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{3,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&=\left[Mc_{k}\delta\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})\right]f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\left\{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}+\gamma_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,4}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\\
&+\left[M\widehat{c}_{k}\delta f_{c,k,7}(\bm{\Phi})+\left\{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}+\gamma_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})\right]\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(521)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{4,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{2\beta\delta^{2}\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}2\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\odot\left(\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right\}\\
+\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right),\end{array}\right\}

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{5,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=2\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\operatorname{Re}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)\\
&+\widehat{{c}}_{k}\delta\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\\
\left.+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\right\}\end{array}\right\},\end{aligned}

(524)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{6,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{2\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)\\
&+\frac{2\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right)\end{array}\right\},\end{aligned}

(525)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{7,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\widehat{{c}}_{k}f_{c,k,6}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,5}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\widehat{{c}}_{k}f_{c,k,5}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,6}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(526)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{8,emi}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&=\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\right)^{2}\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,5}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\frac{2\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,k,5}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right).\end{aligned}

(527)

The gradient of interference is
\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\sum_{\omega=1}^{8}\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{\omega}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}
, where

\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{1}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\gamma_{i}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\mathrm{noise}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}+M^{2}\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta^{2}f_{c,i,7}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+M^{2}\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta^{2}f_{c,k,7}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,i,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),

(528)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{2}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\left\{M\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,k,3}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,i,2}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})f_{c,i,2}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\\
&+\left\{\widehat{c}_{i}\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}f_{c,i,2}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,4}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\\
&+\left\{M\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}+\left\{\widehat{c}_{i}\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,k,3}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,i,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(529)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{3}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&=\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\bm{f}_{c,ki,8}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}f_{c,i,2}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,4}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,i,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi}).\\
&+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta f_{c,ki,8}(\bm{\Phi})+\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})f_{c,i,2}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(530)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{4}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&=\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,ki,8}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\left\{\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})+\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\left(\gamma_{k}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\right\}\bm{f}_{c,ki,8}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(531)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{5}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}\\
&=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}2\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\odot\left(\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right\}\\
+2\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\operatorname{Im}\left\{\bm{\Phi}^{H}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\odot\left(\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)^{T}\right)\bm{c}\right\}+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,i,6}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\end{array}\right\}f_{c,k,5}(\bm{\Phi}).\\
&+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\left|\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\right|^{2}+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,i,6}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,5}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(532)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{6}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=2\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\operatorname{Re}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)\\
&+\widehat{c}_{k}\widehat{c}_{i}\delta\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}{\bf\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}{\bf\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\end{array}\right\},\end{aligned}

(533)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{7}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta^{2}\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,ki,9}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),

(534)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial I_{c,ki}^{8}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{2\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Re}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)\\
&+\frac{\widehat{c}_{i}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\mathbf{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\mathbf{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\right)\\
+\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{i}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right)\end{array}\right\}.\end{aligned}

(535)

The gradient of signal leakage is
\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{leak}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\sum_{\omega=1}^{8}\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{\omega,leak}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}
, where

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{1,\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=M\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\gamma_{k}\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),

(536)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{2,leak}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\left\{M\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}\delta\bm{f}_{c,k,7}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})+\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}\delta\bm{f}_{c,kk,8}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})+\left(\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+2\widehat{c}_{k}\gamma_{k}+\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)\bm{f}_{c,k,4}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\left\{M\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}\delta\left|f_{k}(\bm{\Phi})\right|^{2}+\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}\delta f_{c,kk,8}(\bm{\Phi})+\left(\widehat{c}_{k}^{2}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})+2\widehat{c}_{k}\gamma_{k}+\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right)f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(537)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{3\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\left\{\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,kk,8}(\bm{\Phi})+\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\gamma_{k}+\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\delta\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}\right\}\bm{f}_{c,kk,8}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(538)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{4,\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\left\{\widehat{c}_{k}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})+\left(\gamma_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})\right)\bm{f}_{c,1}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})\right\}f_{c,k,4}(\bm{\Phi})\\
&+\left\{\gamma_{k}^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}\sigma^{2}}{\tau p}+\frac{\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\left(\gamma_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})\right)f_{c,1}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,4}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(539)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{5,\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\delta^{2}\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)},\bm{f}_{c,kk,9}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),

(540)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{6,\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{2\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Re}\left\{\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\mathbf{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\right\}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{M}\right)\\
&+\frac{c_{k}\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\operatorname{Tr}\left\{\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\right\}\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\\
+\mathbf{z}_{k}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}\bm{\Phi}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\mathbf{R}_{ris},\mathbf{R}_{emi}\right)\\
+\bm{f}_{d}\left(\mathbf{R}_{ris}\bm{\Phi}\mathbf{R}_{emi}\bm{\Phi}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2}^{H}\bm{\Upsilon}_{k}^{H}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{2},\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}\overline{\mathbf{h}}_{k}^{H}\right)\end{array}\right\},\end{aligned}

(541)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{7,\mathrm{leak}}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\widehat{c}_{k}\bm{f}_{c,k,2}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})f_{c,k,3}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\beta\delta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\left\{\gamma_{k}+\widehat{c}_{k}f_{c,k,2}(\bm{\Phi})\right\}\bm{f}_{c,k,3}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}),\end{aligned}

(542)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\frac{\partial E_{c,k}^{8,leak}}{\partial\bm{\theta}}=\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}\bm{f}_{c,k,5}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta})f_{c,k,6}(\bm{\Phi})+\frac{\widehat{c}_{k}\beta\sigma_{e}^{2}}{\tau p(\delta+1)}f_{c,k,5}(\bm{\Phi})\bm{f}_{c,k,6}^{\prime}(\bm{\theta}).\end{aligned}

(543)
